- **sai-6-performance**: Audit a change for performance problems — backend, frontend, database, and queue tiers on the diff vs parent (or --full / --path), optional --runtime diagnostics — into openspec/changes/{change-name}/performance.md. Points the homepage plugin at Home.md. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/dashboard.md) · ★1)
